Burns to bare skin 1.9 mi (3.1 km). Initial radiation 100 rad 1.2 mi (1.9 km). Wind from 270° at 18 mph. Military prediction (FM 3-3-1 (1994) / FM 3-11.3 (2009)): Zone I 34 km, Zone II 69 km, toward 90°. PLANNING ESTIMATE. Follow official instructions. Measure radiation with a dose-rate meter. Planning estimate, not a forecast.</description>
